# From Gucci to WPP, marketers are rethinking AI, ad agencies, and the rest is marketing campaigns

![rw-book-cover](https://substackcdn.com/icons/substack/apple-touch-icon-1024x1024.png)

## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: From Gucci to WPP, marketers are rethinking AI, ad agencies, and the rest is marketing campaigns
- Category: #articles
- Summary: Marketers are changing how they use AI and ad agencies after recent chaos and big changes in the industry. Many brands launched new campaigns inspired by culture, sports, and technology. Agencies are also shifting strategies, with cost cuts, new hires, and reorganizations shaping the future of marketing.

##### Shall we have the talk? It’s a post-Olympics marketing recap, filled with chaos, layoffs, restructuring of holding companies, and campaigns that happened in the last two weeks.

It’s Brand + Advertising coverage from 15.02 — 28.02.2026….

> Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!JnHP!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f7feba16-1b6c-46e2-907e-80fe751e6df2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Paramount](https://substack.com/redirect/01c3ad27-dcd0-4524-af51-f7ae863d6621?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) set for [$111bn Warner Bros takeover](https://substack.com/redirect/9e515e49-e544-4c53-836c-04e80cd430ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after Netflix drops bid.
	+ All the ways [Netflix actually won](https://substack.com/redirect/1333be5a-6eb6-41ec-b0a4-a1d6eae40d6a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) even though it lost Warner.
* [WPP](https://substack.com/redirect/db829adc-3091-4ed1-b075-5f7d4b4f145c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) abandons [holding company model](https://substack.com/redirect/815b1b55-3d89-42be-995e-56fa27d4e6fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in [major strategic overhaul](https://substack.com/redirect/e8326214-9a08-4b8d-a1a2-a32d47d6ad12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Omnicom says talent will be the ‘[differentiator](https://substack.com/redirect/1e209208-e9ac-47de-b592-a98b3c6c02cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ but plans $1bn in labor cost cuts.
	+ WPP releases [trove of confidential client data](https://substack.com/redirect/c2b6717d-248b-4eb6-a5ae-d2f4d9428bea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) while fighting suit from ex-employee.
* Shein founder [emerges from shadows](https://substack.com/redirect/cc5c4d3c-fad9-4222-a9d9-8a698119cd21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to hail Chinese roots.
* Nike ad campaign nicknamed Michael Jordan the ‘[Black Mamba](https://substack.com/redirect/84020e48-cc58-4117-8d24-47b828dd0a30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ years before Kobe Bryant adopted the persona.
* After lonely baby monkey goes viral, his [IKEA comfort toy](https://substack.com/redirect/bed46101-9ef1-4050-a095-d6c8bb47c512?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sells out.
* [Charlie Kirk](https://substack.com/redirect/f27abea8-28ca-4b46-bf21-79608ab420de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is all over GOP campaign ads.
* Mumsnet calls for [under-16s social media ban](https://substack.com/redirect/5e462d9f-0598-4405-a4e4-83532d077d21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with cigarette-style health warnings.
* American power rested on culture. [No longer](https://substack.com/redirect/dbbfdaaa-0fa2-46ca-a460-bdeee6f9c185?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Americans are [leaving the U.S.](https://substack.com/redirect/e9b5919f-7fca-42a5-92f1-7d4d5c15b38c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in record numbers.
* Block [lays off nearly half its staff](https://substack.com/redirect/e411daa5-d307-4ce9-92a7-7d0183439e8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) because of AI. Its CEO said most companies will do the same.
* Can [Demna](https://substack.com/redirect/a0258991-1158-4b18-b561-94dd11434027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) save [Gucci](https://substack.com/redirect/f4a80a93-1d91-494a-ab61-229ed7cbbab4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)? Gucci criticised for ‘[AI slop](https://substack.com/redirect/f5abc498-893b-4c8c-98b7-834137d3ca9f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ images ahead of major fashion show.
* You can now book a stay on Airbnb at the famous [Heated Rivalry cottage](https://substack.com/redirect/040a5aa1-9c28-42bf-b347-9b8ec723c239?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* The Soundtrack of the Doomscroll Generation? [It’s Phonk](https://substack.com/redirect/7ed8510a-26e2-4a51-8f69-31b632571dfb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Americans now [listen to podcasts](https://substack.com/redirect/80133c7d-ec28-47e2-8777-15de982eb251?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) more often than talk radio, study shows.
* [Liverpool fans](https://substack.com/redirect/7efb55d8-48eb-440f-a9af-8ef24c93f58b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sign ‘You’ll Never Walk Alone’ in [BSL](https://substack.com/redirect/4de9b1b8-c53f-40a6-889f-05473481882b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as part of new deaf ‘movement.’
* Nestlé, the world’s biggest food company, [doesn’t want to make](https://substack.com/redirect/4a357f58-7bcf-4f84-9301-35d04944ac3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ice cream anymore.
* Brands celebrate [tariff reprieve](https://substack.com/redirect/65cf0d37-abb7-42bb-bc37-ca79b631410c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but fresh uncertainty looms.
* ‘[We’re losing accessibility](https://substack.com/redirect/99abad5c-103e-4c86-98de-b4e8be574cc5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: America says goodbye to the mass-market paperback.
* Literally [every Pokémon got a special logo](https://substack.com/redirect/50769e64-223b-4b0f-ad10-9e11722e74ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Pokémon’s 30th anniversary.
* [6-year-olds](https://substack.com/redirect/00dd0d3e-0e83-4e3f-a8fd-41bb541ae367?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) want makeup. These brands are cashing in.
* There’s [a crisis in non-fiction](https://substack.com/redirect/99403ba1-49a0-47a8-8175-cf75ac05b418?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) book sales. What’s to blame?

* [Samuel L. Jackson](https://substack.com/redirect/77de8b79-f06e-4d1e-b1c9-ad4dd0510e95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) hunts for [Adidas Originals Superstars](https://substack.com/redirect/cff144a3-e070-400e-b221-26231448db5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in a new campaign that stars Lamine Yamal, Baby Keem, James Harden, Tyshawn Jones and Olivia Dean.
* The “Punch the monkey” marketing responses from [IKEA’s different regional branches](https://substack.com/redirect/1a2a7b7c-ffdb-4e6f-8c2c-a9afa88c01e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tell different stories about future of reactionary marketing on social media.
* Old Spice releases ‘[Mom Song 2.0: The End of Adolescents](https://substack.com/redirect/559999e3-5258-4a0d-91ff-1abfe3cba7fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ campaign to target the next generation.
	+ [Folgers](https://substack.com/redirect/ab00fada-3ef7-43e8-910d-68272e928533?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) also turned [its iconic jingle](https://substack.com/redirect/639228f7-7733-47a1-af38-7a6e8a6e50e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into a wake-up anthem for a new generation.
	+ [JID](https://substack.com/redirect/e94378bb-0a93-4458-8d82-2e6afa40f706?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) puts hip-hop spin on iconic Kellogg’s Frosted Flakes “[Hey Tony](https://substack.com/redirect/4966c36d-e8bb-4b99-a244-d795126b78fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” jingle.
* Bosch builds [cute robots](https://substack.com/redirect/cb511ca1-579d-492e-8e04-7a7644184d30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to push windshield wiper innovation.
* [Emma Raducanu](https://substack.com/redirect/7b443ff7-8949-4a0a-80e4-cbfd1b15d622?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) departs Nike for ‘US$3.5m’ [Uniqlo deal](https://substack.com/redirect/33ec6cf4-aef5-48c9-9676-aec20a264d10?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Coach’s Spring 2026 “[Explore Your Story](https://substack.com/redirect/be9d1633-b381-4a2c-ac83-020dea8fa2ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign pairs the Tabby bag with [readable book charms](https://substack.com/redirect/e4c9b31f-d0bf-42e3-8e69-9259f52b11a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) starring Storm Reid, Elle Fanning, and more.
* In new campaign, [Cat in a hat](https://substack.com/redirect/bfbf4695-a059-4cd7-9f1b-503840a8b3d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is TKMaxx’s newest fashion model.
* [IHOP](https://substack.com/redirect/31e199c3-c626-44eb-af6c-d34de6e2f553?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) crowns the first Tuesday of March as National Pancake Day, kicking off with free pancakes & a Mr. Fantasy meet up in Hollywood.
* [Saucony’s 4-minute ad](https://substack.com/redirect/65e538ff-6d47-41cc-ac9a-cb0fe964fabe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) celebrates the community in solo running.
* “This Is An Xbox” campaign “[offended](https://substack.com/redirect/85278395-00cb-43d8-91c2-1cc2944f910f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” employees internally, report claims.
* [All the brands](https://substack.com/redirect/dbcfc820-05d2-4dc1-ab2e-50b43bd80992?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ([and Flavor Flav](https://substack.com/redirect/75cc4a21-f42e-4aba-8b25-1f0bea770d32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)) who rallied behind the U.S. women’s hockey team after Trump’s misogynistic “joke” to the men’s team.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!4793!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff55950f5-b64b-48fa-bae9-f98983448c9b_1924x1259.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/afa76db6-46c4-4eee-a77d-6ea8fe3d6133?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Chappell Roan’s first-ever ad](https://substack.com/redirect/2768cf94-60d6-4c8c-b206-2dcf79e43659?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) campaign is here — Gabbriette and Quenlin Blackwell also appear and share their lip combos to celebrate M.A.C. and Sephora’s new partnership.
* Everything is essential ([and real](https://substack.com/redirect/49735018-6fa8-4e35-aed4-bbf963c013dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)) in [New Omoda 7 campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/de992b67-77ed-4bd6-822f-6f54508e7b7b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by Uncommon.
* Formula 1 and Disney extend “[Fuel the Magic](https://substack.com/redirect/4cb72d4d-f80d-4a7e-984f-a6dcc5d52dcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” campaign, bringing [Mickey & Friends](https://substack.com/redirect/b82ab31f-133e-43d3-bfff-735bc25d775f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to fans.
* Canva [announces](https://substack.com/redirect/0718e2ff-1663-4b2a-b8c2-11650bdd1a0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reality TV star Gemma Collins as its [new UK creative director](https://substack.com/redirect/0f389545-4711-4229-af50-c57c74041680?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in a mockumentary-style campaign.
* [bibigo](https://substack.com/redirect/dec4e789-1ed8-426b-a8b9-791da0e6b04c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reinvents Korean chopsticks to create [ScrollSticks](https://substack.com/redirect/6e06144f-41bd-41dd-b3a2-e962e136fe8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Chopsticks with one side for eating and the other for scrolling.

***The Social Juice+** includes access to newsletter archive and strategy newsletters. **It’s your decision, But you are losing all three benefits**. When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/90fc606d-c66f-47df-ac0a-dbe96c9996d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

*\* **Others:** [Student Discount](https://substack.com/redirect/9ed859d6-c8d4-455c-9955-779151e3ef03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & Localised pricing for [Asia](https://substack.com/redirect/c45442c8-0165-4207-9ab8-f62a81f65da0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [Eastern Europe](https://substack.com/redirect/4cde890a-e8d7-4890-82e0-8e081314f273?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [South America](https://substack.com/redirect/db8e9248-d9d7-4941-a270-daf617e69d39?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & [Africa](https://substack.com/redirect/285bfacc-464c-4396-b4f0-fe8ce9b7bc0e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).* 

*\* I use illustrations from William Heath Robinson for my heading titles, his book William Heath Robinson Inventions is a creative genius. I love it.*

<h1 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;color: rgb(54,55,55);font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 900;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 0;line-height: 36px;font-size: 32px;"><a href="https://substack.com/app-link/post?publication\_id=712314&amp;post\_id=189374084&amp;utm\_source=post-email-title&amp;utm\_campaign=email-post-title&amp;isFreemail=true&amp;r=6q6egl&amp;token=eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjo0MDY3NjU3NDksInBvc3RfaWQiOjE4OTM3NDA4NCwiaWF0IjoxNzcyMzIwODcxLCJleHAiOjE3NzQ5MTI4NzEsImlzcyI6InB1Yi03MTIzMTQiLCJzdWIiOiJwb3N0LXJlYWN0aW9uIn0.f4AEc1nqOMATiGoQ-9fDOOnd2ILWmhBbK29XBbfguRM" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: none;">From Gucci to WPP, marketers are rethinking AI, ad agencies, and the rest is marketing campaigns</a></h1>
<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">Shall we have the talk? It’s a post-Olympics marketing recap, filled with chaos, layoffs, restructuring of holding companies, and campaigns that happened in the last two weeks.</h3>

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">It’s Brand + Advertising coverage from 15.02 — 28.02.2026….</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/65be8b29-bd55-4582-95a9-cf13398a2c95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f7feba16-1b6c-46e2-907e-80fe751e6df2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":67342,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!JnHP!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/01c3ad27-dcd0-4524-af51-f7ae863d6621?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Paramount</a><span> set for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9e515e49-e544-4c53-836c-04e80cd430ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$111bn Warner Bros takeover</a><span> after Netflix drops bid.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>All the ways </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1333be5a-6eb6-41ec-b0a4-a1d6eae40d6a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Netflix actually won</a><span> even though it lost Warner.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/db829adc-3091-4ed1-b075-5f7d4b4f145c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WPP</a><span> abandons </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/815b1b55-3d89-42be-995e-56fa27d4e6fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">holding company model </a><span>in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e8326214-9a08-4b8d-a1a2-a32d47d6ad12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">major strategic overhaul</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Omnicom says talent will be the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1e209208-e9ac-47de-b592-a98b3c6c02cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">differentiator</a><span>,’ but plans $1bn in labor cost cuts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WPP releases </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2b6717d-248b-4eb6-a5ae-d2f4d9428bea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">trove of confidential client data</a><span> while fighting suit from ex-employee.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Shein founder </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cc5c4d3c-fad9-4222-a9d9-8a698119cd21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">emerges from shadows</a><span> to hail Chinese roots.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nike ad campaign nicknamed Michael Jordan the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/84020e48-cc58-4117-8d24-47b828dd0a30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Black Mamba</a><span>’ years before Kobe Bryant adopted the persona.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>After lonely baby monkey goes viral, his </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bed46101-9ef1-4050-a095-d6c8bb47c512?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IKEA comfort toy</a><span> sells out.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f27abea8-28ca-4b46-bf21-79608ab420de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Charlie Kirk</a><span> is all over GOP campaign ads.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Mumsnet calls for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5e462d9f-0598-4405-a4e4-83532d077d21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">under-16s social media ban</a><span> with cigarette-style health warnings.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>American power rested on culture. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dbbfdaaa-0fa2-46ca-a460-bdeee6f9c185?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">No longer</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Americans are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e9b5919f-7fca-42a5-92f1-7d4d5c15b38c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">leaving the U.S. </a><span>in record numbers.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Block </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e411daa5-d307-4ce9-92a7-7d0183439e8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lays off nearly half its staff</a><span> because of AI. Its CEO said most companies will do the same.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a0258991-1158-4b18-b561-94dd11434027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Demna</a><span> save </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f4a80a93-1d91-494a-ab61-229ed7cbbab4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gucci</a><span>? Gucci criticised for ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f5abc498-893b-4c8c-98b7-834137d3ca9f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI slop</a><span>’ images ahead of major fashion show.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>You can now book a stay on Airbnb at the famous </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/040a5aa1-9c28-42bf-b347-9b8ec723c239?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Heated Rivalry cottage</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Soundtrack of the Doomscroll Generation? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ed8510a-26e2-4a51-8f69-31b632571dfb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">It’s Phonk</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Americans now </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/80133c7d-ec28-47e2-8777-15de982eb251?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">listen to podcasts</a><span> more often than talk radio, study shows.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7efb55d8-48eb-440f-a9af-8ef24c93f58b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Liverpool fans</a><span> sign ‘You’ll Never Walk Alone’ in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4de9b1b8-c53f-40a6-889f-05473481882b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">BSL</a><span> as part of new deaf ‘movement.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nestlé, the world’s biggest food company, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4a357f58-7bcf-4f84-9301-35d04944ac3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">doesn’t want to make</a><span> ice cream anymore.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands celebrate </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/65cf0d37-abb7-42bb-bc37-ca79b631410c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tariff reprieve</a><span>, but fresh uncertainty looms.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99abad5c-103e-4c86-98de-b4e8be574cc5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">We’re losing accessibility</a><span>’: America says goodbye to the mass-market paperback.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Literally </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/50769e64-223b-4b0f-ad10-9e11722e74ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">every Pokémon got a special logo</a><span> for Pokémon’s 30th anniversary.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/00dd0d3e-0e83-4e3f-a8fd-41bb541ae367?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">6-year-olds</a><span> want makeup. These brands are cashing in.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>There’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99403ba1-49a0-47a8-8175-cf75ac05b418?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a crisis in non-fiction</a><span> book sales. What’s to blame?</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5964cfd8-cb45-446e-932b-89fd8811cfdc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":65322,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!\_L36!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/664de0c5-378e-4286-b9d3-166cab252630?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">best</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59f03428-d0ec-4227-aece-691adaf242b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rest of marketing campaigns</a><span> at 2026 Winter Olympics.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/77de8b79-f06e-4d1e-b1c9-ad4dd0510e95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Samuel L. Jackson</a><span> hunts for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cff144a3-e070-400e-b221-26231448db5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Adidas Originals Superstars</a><span> in a new campaign that stars Lamine Yamal, Baby Keem, James Harden, Tyshawn Jones and Olivia Dean.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The “Punch the monkey” marketing responses from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a2a7b7c-ffdb-4e6f-8c2c-a9afa88c01e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IKEA’s different regional branches</a><span> tell different stories about future of reactionary marketing on social media. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Old Spice releases ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/559999e3-5258-4a0d-91ff-1abfe3cba7fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mom Song 2.0: The End of Adolescents</a><span>’ campaign to target the next generation.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ab00fada-3ef7-43e8-910d-68272e928533?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Folgers</a><span> also turned </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/639228f7-7733-47a1-af38-7a6e8a6e50e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">its iconic jingle</a><span> into a wake-up anthem for a new generation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e94378bb-0a93-4458-8d82-2e6afa40f706?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">JID</a><span> puts hip-hop spin on iconic Kellogg’s Frosted Flakes “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4966c36d-e8bb-4b99-a244-d795126b78fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hey Tony</a><span>” jingle.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Bosch builds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb511ca1-579d-492e-8e04-7a7644184d30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cute robots</a><span> to push windshield wiper innovation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b443ff7-8949-4a0a-80e4-cbfd1b15d622?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Emma Raducanu</a><span> departs Nike for ‘US$3.5m’ </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33ec6cf4-aef5-48c9-9676-aec20a264d10?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Uniqlo deal</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Coach’s Spring 2026 “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/be9d1633-b381-4a2c-ac83-020dea8fa2ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Explore Your Story</a><span>” campaign pairs the Tabby bag with</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4c9b31f-d0bf-42e3-8e69-9259f52b11a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> readable book charms</a><span> starring Storm Reid, Elle Fanning, and more.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>In new campaign, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bfbf4695-a059-4cd7-9f1b-503840a8b3d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Cat in a hat</a><span> is TKMaxx’s newest fashion model.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/31e199c3-c626-44eb-af6c-d34de6e2f553?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IHOP</a><span> crowns the first Tuesday of March as National Pancake Day, kicking off with free pancakes &amp; a Mr. Fantasy meet up in Hollywood.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/65e538ff-6d47-41cc-ac9a-cb0fe964fabe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Saucony’s 4-minute ad</a><span> celebrates the community in solo running.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“This Is An Xbox” campaign “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/85278395-00cb-43d8-91c2-1cc2944f910f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">offended</a><span>” employees internally, report claims.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dbcfc820-05d2-4dc1-ab2e-50b43bd80992?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">All the brands</a><span> (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75cc4a21-f42e-4aba-8b25-1f0bea770d32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">and Flavor Flav</a><span>) who rallied behind the U.S. women’s hockey team after Trump’s misogynistic “joke” to the men’s team.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="675"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/afa76db6-46c4-4eee-a77d-6ea8fe3d6133?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/f55950f5-b64b-48fa-bae9-f98983448c9b\_1924x1259.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":953,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":675,"bytes":763747,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/189374084?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff55950f5-b64b-48fa-bae9-f98983448c9b\_1924x1259.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="359.99313186813185" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!4793!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff55950f5-b64b-48fa-bae9-f98983448c9b\_1924x1259.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2768cf94-60d6-4c8c-b206-2dcf79e43659?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Chappell Roan’s first-ever ad </a><span>campaign is here — Gabbriette and Quenlin Blackwell also appear and share their lip combos to celebrate M.A.C. and Sephora’s new partnership.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Everything is essential (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49735018-6fa8-4e35-aed4-bbf963c013dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">and real</a><span>) in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de992b67-77ed-4bd6-822f-6f54508e7b7b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New Omoda 7 campaign</a><span> by Uncommon.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Formula 1 and Disney extend “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4cb72d4d-f80d-4a7e-984f-a6dcc5d52dcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Fuel the Magic</a><span>” campaign, bringing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b82ab31f-133e-43d3-bfff-735bc25d775f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mickey &amp; Friends</a><span> to fans.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Canva </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0718e2ff-1663-4b2a-b8c2-11650bdd1a0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">announces</a><span> reality TV star Gemma Collins as its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0f389545-4711-4229-af50-c57c74041680?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new UK creative director</a><span> in a mockumentary-style campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dec4e789-1ed8-426b-a8b9-791da0e6b04c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bibigo</a><span> reinvents Korean chopsticks to create </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e06144f-41bd-41dd-b3a2-e962e136fe8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ScrollSticks</a><span>: Chopsticks with one side for eating and the other for scrolling. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Capcom’s Resident Evil Requiem ad campaign </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a51f5787-d4b0-42a4-b732-5dbe19f4c5f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">involves</a><span> a faux lawsuit against </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4b4dd483-e343-466c-9a54-d3eaf1089f2f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Nissin Foods</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5a4c4941-8d01-4333-80b1-1985fa606207?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Spotify</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5ac00928-4273-41c8-be5f-202cce7c061a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Liquid Death</a><span> have revealed the world’s first music-streaming urn.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6d6b21d9-70a3-4351-8d9f-283d561ec485?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Telstra</a><span> turns </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6e5ed32-802d-48f3-a64e-bdc70568bc07?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">e-waste into a synthesizer</a><span> to raise awareness of the discarded electronics in landfills and collecting dust.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/83bdc294-cf4b-4ac0-ad4e-0024a3d9c7b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Maria Grazia Chiuri</a><span> lets the fur fly at Fendi.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Selena Gomez’s reacts to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9580acdb-3d5a-4bde-9861-339529512727?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Alysa Liu wearing Rare Beauty</a><span> at the 2026 Winter Olympics.</span></p></li>
